### [1] [Web-Native Graphical EMF Model Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11442)
*Susanne Göbel,Ralf Lämmel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EMFular，一个纯Web框架，用于从Ecore模型生成图形化编辑器，支持EMF一致性、无后端部署、Angular生态扩展，并在编辑能力、可定制性和鲁棒性方面进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形化模型编辑正从桌面应用转向Web工具，现有框架缺乏低门槛生成、高可定制性和便捷部署的平衡设计，亟需一种纯Web、无后端的EMF模型管理方案。

Method: 分析现有框架特征，提炼设计原则（低门槛生成、广泛定制、便捷部署）；开发EMFular框架，包含EMFular生成器（Ecore模型→可定制编辑器），编辑器基于Angular构建并提供扩展点，实现EMF一致性（创建、检视、导航、编辑、撤销/重做、包含/反向引用处理）及与现有EMF工具的互操作性（兼容序列化/反序列化）。

Result: EMFular可生成具备完整建模操作、EMF一致性、EMF工具互操作性的图形化编辑器；评估验证了编辑器充分性（可用编辑能力）、适应性（定制机制及所需工作量）和生成鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EMFular实现了纯Web、无后端的EMF模型图形化编辑，兼顾低门槛生成与高度可定制性，通过Angular生态扩展保障了编辑器的充分性、适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graphical model editing is shifting from desktop applications to web-based tools. We analyze the characteristics of existing frameworks and, based on this analysis, we derive a set of design principles that imply low-effort generation, extensive customization possibilities, and straightforward deployment of the resulting editors. On these grounds, we introduce EMFular, a purely web-based framework for managing EMF models without any backend. The accompanying EMFular generator maps a given Ecore model (an EMF metamodel) to a ready-to-use and ready-to-customize graphical editor. EMFular editors provide 'EMF consistency', that is, they not only support standard modeling operations such as creation, inspection, navigation, editing, and undo/redo, but they also handle containment and inverse references in close alignment with EMF; they also provide interoperability with existing EMF tooling through compatible de-/serialization. A generated editor is an Angular project with designated extension points, which allows developers to customize and extend all aspects of the editor using the expressive power of Angular and its ecosystem, guided by the extension points of EMFular. We evaluate EMFular in terms of editor adequacy (available editing capabilities), adaptability (customization mechanisms and required effort), and robustness of the generation.

</details>


### [2] [Defeater Cards: Characterizing and Managing Safety Assurance Case Defeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11462)
*Usman Gohar,Michael C. Hunter,Salil Purandare,Jordan J. Rios,Myra B. Cohen,Robyn R. Lutz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Defeater Cards（反驳卡）——一种结构化文档工件，用于在安全论证案例中系统化地表征、推理和管理反驳因素。基于文献调查和主题分析，结合5W1H框架确定文档标准，并通过两个跨领域案例研究验证其有效性，同时开源了反驳卡库以支持社区复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全论证案例中的反驳因素（defeaters）缺乏结构化支持，存在定义不一致、难以审查、缺乏文档标准和可复用性差等问题，亟需一种系统化的方法来表征和管理反驳因素。

Method: 通过文献调查和主题分析，基于5W1H框架（What, Why, Who, Where, When, How）识别文档标准，设计Defeater Cards结构化工件，并通过两个跨领域案例研究验证其适用性。

Result: Defeater Cards能够揭示隐藏假设、暴露推理漏洞、支持安全论证案例的持续演化，并提升了可追溯性和可审计性。开源库的发布促进了社区协作与知识复用。

Conclusion: Defeater Cards为安全论证案例中的反驳因素管理提供了一种标准化、结构化且可复用的解决方案，有助于提升安全关键系统的论证质量和审查效率。

Abstract: Safety assurance cases provide structured justifications that safety-critical systems meet their safety requirements. Recently, the notion of defeaters has emerged as a rigorous means of challenging the validity of safety arguments. Examples of defeaters might include overly strict claims, unreliable evidence, or reasoning gaps. However, defeaters remain ad hoc, lack structured support for critical reflection, are inconsistently described, are difficult to review, and lack documentation standards. To address this, we propose Defeater Cards, a new structured documentation artifact for systematically characterizing, reasoning about, and managing defeaters in safety cases. Drawing on a literature survey and thematic analysis, we identify documentation criteria that inform the card's structure, based on the 5W1H framework. Defeater Cards are designed to support informed analysis and evolution, improve traceability and auditability, and enable the reuse of defeater knowledge across systems and product variants. We demonstrate their applicability through two cross-domain case studies, showing how they expose hidden assumptions, surface reasoning gaps, and support ongoing safety assurance case evolution. To support adoption and community reuse, we also release an open-source repository of defeater cards as a baseline upon which researchers and practitioners can build and describe lessons learned.

</details>


### [3] [SentTrack: Sentiment-Driven Bottleneck Detection in GitHub Issue Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11476)
*Xinyu Hu,Ali Behbahani,Daniel Moon,Yaren Dogan,Nasir U. Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SentTrack是一个双通道框架，用于从GitHub Issue讨论中检测社会技术瓶颈。通过对AvaloniaUI开源仓库约9000个Issue线程的分析，发现49%的线程陷入停滞，仅13%得到解决。框架结合水平管道（LLM摘要+语义聚类）和垂直管道（ABCDE协作交互框架），并设计了加权评分引擎用于可解释的优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程团队越来越依赖GitHub Issue线程来协调工作、报告错误和协商技术决策，但大多数仓库健康工具仅关注代码指标，忽略了驱动或阻碍开发的对话动态。

Method: SentTrack结合两条互补管道：水平管道使用大语言模型将原始Issue报告转化为简洁摘要，提取中层关注短语，并通过UMAP和HDBSCAN进行聚类；垂直管道应用ABCDE协作交互框架对每条评论进行分类并推断线程级结果。加权评分引擎结合消极性、停滞、解决差距和线程长度四个指标。

Result: 在AvaloniaUI开源仓库约9000个Issue线程上应用，前3608个Issue产生了613个语义聚类。全语料中49%的线程以停滞结束，仅13%达到解决，解决差距被识别为主导瓶颈信号。

Conclusion: SentTrack为维护者提供了一个可解释的优先级排序工具，可在讨论停滞之前识别高摩擦的讨论线程，有效补充了现有仅关注代码指标的仓库健康工具。

Abstract: Software engineering teams increasingly depend on GitHub issue threads to coordinate work, report bugs, and negotiate technical decisions, yet most repository health tools focus on code metrics and ignore the conversational dynamics that drive or stall development. This paper presents SentTrack, a dual-lens framework for detecting socio-technical bottlenecks from GitHub issue discussions. Applied to the AvaloniaUI open-source repository across approximately 9,000 issue threads, the framework addresses three questions: how to automate workflow-inefficiency detection from real-time conversational data, whether sentiment signals can surface risk earlier than traditional label-based methods, and how to isolate human narrative from machine-generated noise in mixed-media issue text. SentTrack combines two complementary pipelines. A horizontal pipeline translates raw issue reports into clean summaries using a large language model, extracts mid-level concern phrases, and clusters them through UMAP and HDBSCAN, producing 613 semantic clusters from the first 3,608 issues processed. A vertical pipeline applies the ABCDE collaborative interaction framework to classify each comment and infer thread-level outcomes. Across the full corpus, 49\% of threads ended in stagnation and only 13\% reached resolution, with the resolution gap identified as the dominant bottleneck signal. A weighted scoring engine that combines negativity, stagnation, resolution gap, and thread length gives maintainers an interpretable prioritization tool for high-friction discussions before they stall development.

</details>


### [4] [Acoda: Adversarial Code Obfuscation for Defending against LLM-based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11755)
*Hongzhou Rao,Zikan Dong,Yanjie Zhao,Haodong Li,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Acoda，一种基于遗传算法的对抗性代码混淆框架，通过8种保持语义的混淆方法结合遗传算法迭代优化，有效防御LLM对源代码的分析，在7个SOTA LLM上实现最高70%的攻击成功率，并具有良好的跨模型迁移性和低运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程任务中的广泛应用（代码理解、调试、漏洞检测等）带来了新的安全与隐私风险——LLM能够分析、重构甚至逆向工程源代码逻辑，可能导致知识产权泄露。

Method: 利用LLM的安全对齐机制和基于token的信息处理两个关键特性，设计8种保持语义的混淆方法；通过遗传算法迭代优化混淆策略以生成最大化防御效果的对抗样本；提出基于LLM响应的定量评估框架，结合辅助LLM和四项评估指标综合评价。

Result: 在GPT-4o、DeepSeek、Qwen、Llama、Gemma等7个SOTA LLM上，Acoda实现了最高70%的攻击成功率（ASR），具有较强的跨模型迁移性和极低的运行时开销，同时保证原始代码语义不变。

Conclusion: Acoda为LLM时代的代码保护和LLM安全防御提供了新的视角，通过对抗性混淆有效诱导LLM拒绝或错误分析代码。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software engineering (SE) tasks such as code understanding, debugging, and vulnerability detection, their powerful semantic reasoning ability has also introduced new security and privacy risks. LLMs can analyze, reconstruct, or even reverse-engineer source code logic, potentially leading to the leakage of intellectual property. To address this issue, we propose Acoda, a genetic algorithm-based adversarial code obfuscation framework that defends against LLM-based code analysis. Acoda leverages two key mechanisms of LLMs, namely safety alignment and token-based information processing, to design 8 semantics-preserving obfuscation methods. It iteratively optimizes obfuscation strategies through a genetic algorithm to generate adversarial samples that maximize defensive effectiveness. In addition, we propose a quantitative evaluation framework based on LLM responses, which combines an auxiliary LLM and four evaluation metrics to assess how target LLMs analyze obfuscated code comprehensively. Experimental results show that Acoda can effectively induce LLMs to refuse or misinterpret code analysis. On 7 state-of-the-art LLMs, including GPT-4o, DeepSeek, Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, Acoda achieves an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 70%, with strong cross-model transferability and minimal runtime overhead, while ensuring that the semantics of the original code remain unchanged. Overall, this study provides a new perspective for code protection and LLM security defense in the era of LLMs.

</details>


### [5] [Understanding and Detecting Scalability Faults in Large-Scale Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11815)
*Hao-Nan Zhu,Goodness Ayinmode,Cesar A. Stuardo,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Cindy Rubio-González*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对大规模分布式系统中的可扩展性故障进行了系统性研究，分析了444个可扩展性问题报告，发现了可扩展性故障主要由维度代码片段与其相关的反模式协同引起。基于此，提出了ScaleLens方法，结合动态与静态分析检测可扩展性故障，实验表明其检测效果优于基线方法4.2倍，并在Cassandra、HDFS和Ignite的最新稳定版本中发现了334个存在确认问题的维度代码片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式系统规模的增长，系统复杂性可能导致可扩展性故障。这类故障具有潜伏性且只在大规模部署时才会显现，难以发现和诊断。目前缺乏对该类故障的全面系统性研究，因此需要深入理解其根因和反模式，并提出有效的检测方法。

Method: 首先对10个大规模分布式系统中的444个可扩展性问题报告进行系统性分析，归纳可扩展性故障的常见反模式和根因。然后基于分析发现，设计并实现了ScaleLens方法，该方法结合动态分析（运行时监测资源使用模式）和静态分析（代码结构分析），识别维度代码片段并将其与已知反模式进行匹配，从而检测可扩展性故障。

Result: ScaleLens检测维度代码片段的效率是基线方法的4.2倍。在Cassandra、HDFS和Ignite的最新稳定版本中，ScaleLens检测到334个维度代码片段，这些片段均具有确认的问题行为。

Conclusion: 论文表明，大多数可扩展性故障是由于维度代码片段与相关反模式的协同作用导致的。结合动态和静态分析的ScaleLens方法能有效检测这类故障，为提升大规模分布式系统的可扩展性提供了可行的检测手段。

Abstract: Scalable distributed systems form the backbone of modern computing infrastructure. However, as scale grows, system complexity may lead to scalability faults. Scalability faults are challenging to uncover and diagnose, as they are often latent and only manifest at large-scale deployment. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive study on scalability faults and propose an approach for their detection. First, we systematically investigate 444 scalability issue reports from 10 large-scale distributed systems to understand the common anti-patterns and root causes of scalability faults. We found that the majority of these faults are caused by the synergy between dimensional code fragments and anti-patterns associated with them. Second, based on our findings, we design and implement ScaleLens, a novel approach to detect scalability faults. ScaleLens combines dynamic and static analyses to pinpoint dimensional code fragments and match them with anti-patterns. Our evaluation shows that ScaleLens detects 4.2x more dimensional code fragments associated with known scalability faults compared to the baseline. On the latest stable versions of Cassandra, HDFS, and Ignite, ScaleLens detects 334 dimensional code fragments with confirmed problematic behavior.

</details>


### [6] [How Requirements Quality Makes (or Breaks) Traceability Link Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11834)
*Tobias Hey,Julian Frattini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了需求质量缺陷对需求-源代码追踪链接恢复（TLR）性能的影响，发现某些缺陷（如不以名词短语开头的句子）会损害TLR性能，而另一些（如包含实现细节）反而有益，且不同TLR方法对缺陷的敏感度各异，因此TLR方法的最优选择取决于数据集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探索了自动化追踪链接恢复方法，但需求中的质量缺陷如何影响TLR性能尚缺乏实证证据。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统性地量化不同类型需求缺陷对TLR性能的影响。

Method: 在来自两个数据集的189个用例描述中标注了28种质量缺陷类型，采用五种不同的TLR方法进行追踪链接恢复，通过统计检验量化各类缺陷对TLR性能的影响强度。

Result: 部分质量缺陷（如不以名词短语开头的句子）显著损害TLR性能，而另一些缺陷（如包含实现细节）反而提升性能；不同类型的TLR方法对同一缺陷表现出不同的响应模式。

Conclusion: TLR方法的最优选择依赖于数据集的质量特性，即需求缺陷的类型和分布会影响不同方法的相对表现。

Abstract: Traceability information between requirements and source code greatly benefits the maintenance of a software system. Since manually establishing trace links is cumbersome and error-prone, previous research explored automated traceability link recovery (TLR) approaches to support this task. However, quality defects in requirements impact subsequent activities such as TLR, yet evidence about this remains scarce. Our objective is to contribute empirical evidence on this impact. At the same time, we aim to understand how the performance of TLR approaches varies given these quality defects. To this end, we annotated 28 types of quality defect in 189 use case descriptions from two datasets. Then, we executed five distinct TLR approaches on the dataset and measured their performance in recovering trace links. Finally, we performed statistical tests to quantify the defects' effect strength on this performance. Our results show that some quality defects harm TLR performance, e.g., sentences that do not start with noun phrases, while others actually benefit performance, e.g., use cases that include implementation details. Moreover, different types of approaches respond differently to these defects. As a consequence, the performance-optimizing choice of a TLR approach depends on the quality of the dataset.

</details>


### [7] [Enhancing LLM-Based Code Translation with Verified Multi-Semantic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11863)
*Yufu Wang,He Jiang,Hao Lin,Peiyu Zou,Ang Jia,Xiaochen Li,Zhilei Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Multisage框架，通过多语义增强和自校准机制提升LLM代码翻译的可靠性，在HumanEval-X基准上将翻译成功率提升高达2.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码翻译方法依赖token级统计模式而非程序语义理解，导致翻译结果存在逻辑和语义错误；高质量的语义指导资源（如功能描述和测试用例）在实际场景中往往不可用，亟需从源代码中直接构建可靠语义信息的方法。

Method: Multisage包含三个模块：(1) 语义表示解析模块：从源代码提取数据流图、类型约束、外部API信息等结构化基础语义；(2) 多语义增强模块：生成代码摘要、函数级测试用例、API级描述与测试等多种增强语义；(3) 语义一致性校准模块：通过语义保持变异和跨语义一致性验证来过滤、校准和精炼生成的语义。

Result: 在HumanEval-X代码翻译基准上，Multisage将翻译成功率提升高达2.22倍，一致优于vanilla prompting、指令微调LLM和Chain-of-Thought推理方法，在较小模型上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式的语义增强可以显著提升LLM代码翻译的可靠性，通过从源代码构建多种语义表征并进行一致性校准，能够有效弥补高质量语义资源缺失的问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown great promise for automated code translation, yet existing approaches often rely on token-level statistical patterns rather than sufficient understanding of program semantics. As a result, translated programs may still contain logical and semantic errors. Although high-quality semantic guidance, such as functional descriptions and test cases, can help mitigate these errors, such resources are often unavailable in real-world scenarios. This raises two key challenges: how to construct rich semantic information directly from source code, and how to ensure that such semantics are accurate and reliable enough to guide translation.To address these challenges, we propose Multisage, a multi-semantic augmentation and self-calibration framework for LLM-based code translation. Multisage consists of three modules. First, a semantic representation parsing module extracts structured base semantics from source code, including data-flow graphs, type constraints, and external API information. Second, a multi-semantic augmentation module builds on these representations to generate diverse augmented semantics, including code summaries, function-level test cases, and API-oriented descriptions and tests. Third, a semantic consistency calibration module uses semantics-preserving mutations and cross-semantic consistency verification to filter, calibrate, and refine the generated semantics.Experiments on the HumanEval-X code translation benchmark show that Multisage improves translation success rates by up to 2.22 times across diverse backbone models. It consistently outperforms vanilla prompting, instruction-tuned LLMs, and Chain-of-Thought reasoning, with the largest gains observed on smaller models. These results demonstrate that explicit semantic augmentation can substantially improve the reliability of LLM-based code translation.

</details>


### [8] [Agents All the Way Down; A Methodology for Building Custom AI Agents from Substrate to Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11869)
*Marc Alier Forment,Juanan Pereira,Francisco José García-Peñalvo,María José Casañ Guerrero*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套名为"Agents All the Way Down"的方法论，用于端到端构建定制AI代理（Custom AI Agents）。该方法论包含两个前提条件（Substrate和Building blocks）和三个实践循环（P3-P5），并通过AAC项目（基于LAMB平台，由一名开发者在约10天内完成）进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一套端到端构建定制AI代理的规范实践。现有的零散技术（如函数调用API、Model Context Protocol、代码代理等）分散在播客、博客和泄露的系统提示中，没有形成可转移的方法论。

Method: 提出"Agents All the Way Down"方法论：两个前提条件——(P1) Substrate（将LLM视为软件组件，按工具→系统→消息→提示缓存的框架组织）和(P2) Building blocks（函数调用、MCP、CLI编排、liteshell模式、代理循环、技能、角色、钩子和脚手架）；三个实践循环——(P3) 用通用代理原型化、(P4) 收割、折叠并以CLI形式交付（Turtle模式）、(P5) 代理测试代理（用通用代理驱动行为场景测试）。该方法论无框架依赖，多代理编排被视为CLI组合。

Result: 该方法论从AAC项目（LAMB平台的自定义代理）中提炼而成，由一名开发者在约10天内借助AI结对编程完成并投入生产。它被呈现为一种可转移的实践，独立于任何语言或框架。

Conclusion: 定制AI代理的核心区别在于适配性而非能力，每个代理为单一任务而构建，由负责维护的工程师完成。"Agents All the Way Down"方法论填补了端到端构建定制代理的实践空白，且工作循环（P3→P4→P5→P3）自然衍生出一个推论：多代理编排即CLI组合。

Abstract: Custom AI agents areagents that live inside their own
  application, talk to their own data and tools, enforce their own security boundaries,
  and carry their own brand and audit trail. What separates them from the general-purpose
  tier is fit, not capability: each is built for one job, by the
  engineer who will maintain it. No published practice sets out how to build one end to
  end. The pieces are everywhere (function-calling APIs, the Model Context Protocol, code
  agents to pair with), but the practice that chains them lives in podcasts, blogs, and
  leaked system prompts. This paper writes that practice down as a methodology, Agents All
  the Way Down: two preconditions crossed once and kept, then three practices repeated
  for the agent's life. The preconditions are (P1) Substrate, the LLM as a software
  component, framed as tools, then system, then messages under prompt-caching; and (P2)
  Building blocks: function calling, MCP, CLI orchestration, the liteshell pattern, the
  agent loop, skills, characters, hooks, and scaffolding. The practices are (P3) prototype
  with a general-purpose agent; (P4) harvest, fold, and ship the result as a CLI, the
  Turtle pattern; and (P5) agent-tests-agent, in which a general-purpose agent drives it
  through behavioural scenarios, a complement to classical testing, not a replacement. The
  working loop is P3 to P4 to P5 and back, and one corollary falls out for free:
  multi-agent orchestration is just CLI composition. The methodology is framework-free by
  construction. It was distilled from the AAC, a custom agent for the open-source LAMB
  platform, built in about ten days by one developer with an AI pair-programmer and in
  production . We present it as a transferable practice, independent of any language or framework.

</details>


### [9] [Characterizing Software Aging in GPU-Based LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11916)
*Domenico Cotroneo,Bojan Cukic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于研究基于GPU的LLM服务系统中软件老化问题的实证方法论，通过216小时、六组共置部署的对照实验，发现所有部署均存在统计显著的显存老化现象，且泄漏率高度依赖运行时与部署配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件老化研究聚焦于CPU主导、工作负载相对规律的软件系统，而LLM服务系统具有异构架构（Python主机+CUDA设备）、请求成本跨数量级变化、软件栈快速演进等独特特征，现有方法无法直接适用。

Method: 在六组共置部署上运行216小时完全相同的压力实验，并行监控主机、设备和客户端指标，应用考虑了自相关性和多重比较问题的统计推断流程。

Result: 所有部署均检测到统计显著的显存老化，泄漏率与具体的服务运行时和部署配置密切相关。

Conclusion: 提供了可复现的研究框架，开辟了软件老化与再生领域和LLM服务领域交叉的新研究方向。

Abstract: This paper proposes an empirical methodology to study software aging in GPU-based LLM serving systems. Traditional aging studies focus on CPU-centric software with relatively regular workloads; LLM serving is different, spanning a Python host and a CUDA device, handling requests whose cost varies by orders of magnitude, and relying on rapidly evolving software stacks. We run a 216-hour campaign across six co-located deployments under identical stress conditions, monitor host, device, and client metrics in parallel, and apply a statistical pipeline that accounts for autocorrelation and multiple testing. Our results reveal statistically significant memory aging in all deployments, with leak rates strongly dependent on the serving runtime and deployment configuration. Beyond these findings, we provide a reproducible framework that opens a research direction at the intersection of the software aging and rejuvenation and LLM serving communities.

</details>


### [10] [Exploration Structure in LLM Agents for Multi-File Change Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11976)
*Akeela Darryl Fattha,Kia Ying Chua,Lingxiao Jiang,Laura Wynter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了线性顺序探索与非线性领域范围并行智能体探索在软件工程文件定位中的效果。领域并行智能体（使用Haiku-class模型）在微F1分数上显著优于同类模型，仅次于更大的Codex 5.5 High。研究发现文档演化是潜在依赖问题，朴素文件系统访问可能因过度预测测试文件而降低定位质量，强制多智能体协商无显著帮助且大幅增加token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件工程工具大多采用线性顺序方式探索代码仓库，但这种方式对于涉及多个子系统的跨域变更存在结构不匹配问题。作者旨在验证非线性、领域范围的并行智能体探索是否能更有效地定位需要修改的文件。

Method: 在SWE Bench Pro基准测试上（聚焦ansible项目），构建基于持久会话的GitHub问题评估方法（锚定单一基础提交）。对比系统包括：无直接仓库访问的基线LLM、单智能体RLM（带持久Python REPL）、外部CLI基线（Codex 5.5 High）以及领域范围并行智能体文件遍历系统（使用Haiku-class小模型）。

Result: 领域并行智能体在Haiku-class模型中微F1分数大幅领先；在包含2025-2026年PR的扩展基准中仅次于Codex 5.5 High；在原始SWE-bench Pro（2020）上，更大的Sonnet基线LLM因预测文件较少而获得更高精度，但全金标召回率显著更低。

Conclusion: 领域范围并行智能体探索在文件定位任务上优于线性探索方法，特别是对于跨域变更。三个额外发现：文档演化是未被解决的关键依赖；朴素文件系统访问因过度预测测试文件而有害；强制多智能体协商无实际帮助且成本高昂。

Abstract: Software engineering tools increasingly rely on LLM based agents to localize files to change to resolve a software issue. Most AI agents explore repositories linearly, that is, visiting one directory or file per step. We postulate that this is a structural mismatch for changes that span several subsystems. We compare linear sequential exploration against non-linear, domain-scoped parallel agentic exploration. Using SWE Bench Pro as initial benchmark, we focus on ansible as an exemplar. We construct an approach for persistent-session evaluation of GitHub issues anchored at a single base commit. We compare our non-linear domain-agent file traversal system against a base LLM without direct repository access, a single agent Recursive Language Model (RLM) baseline with a persistent Python REPL and an external CLI baseline using Codex 5.5 High. Domain scoped parallel agent spawning with a small Haiku-class model achieves the highest micro F1 among Haiku class models by a large margin. Domain-agents is the second highest behind only the much larger Codex 5.5 High on our own expanded benchmark including over more recent PRs from 2025 and 2026. On the original, curated, 2020 SWE-bench Pro benchmark, a larger Sonnet plain LLM baseline attains higher micro F1 by predicting few files, leading to higher precision, but at significantly lower all gold recall. We also present three additional findings. First, documentation evolution is a latent dependency unresolved by any approach. Second, naive file system access can degrade localization driven by test-file over prediction. Lastly, forced multi-agent consultation does not measurably help and raises token cost substantially.

</details>


### [11] [Undefined Behavior in C and C++: An Experiment With Desktop Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12064)
*Jukka Ruohonen,Krzysztof Sierszecki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过实证实验发现，在典型Linux桌面使用中，C/C++程序中的未定义行为非常普遍。完成59个简单任务生成了近11000个独特的未定义行为警告，涉及32个程序/库，其中大多数与Mesa图形库和GUI交互相关，仅登录GNOME就产生500多个警告，绝大多数警告与虚表指针有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究C/C++程序中未定义行为在典型桌面使用环境下的实际发生频率和分布情况，填补该领域的实证研究空白。

Method: 基于编译器实现的未定义行为消毒器（Undefined Behavior Sanitizer），在Linux发行版上设计并执行59个简单的实验任务，收集并分析产生的未定义行为警告。

Result: 共生成近11000个独特警告，来自32个独特的C/C++程序或库；大多数警告与Mesa图形库和GUI交互相关；仅登录GNOME桌面环境就产生500多个独特警告；绝大多数警告涉及虚表指针；相关堆栈跟踪普遍较长。

Conclusion: 未定义行为在C/C++程序的典型桌面使用中十分常见，该发现为C/C++语言的实证研究文献提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: Undefined behavior is idiomatic to C and C++ programming; such behavior is a use of an erroneous program construct for which the languages impose no requirements, such as integer overflows. The paper presents an empirical experiment seeking to probe the extent of undefined behavior executing underneath typical desktop use of a Linux distribution. The analysis is based on an undefined behavior sanitizer implemented in a compiler. According to the results, undefined behavior is common. By completing 59 simple experimental tasks, nearly 11 thousand unique undefined behavior warnings were generated by 32 unique programs and libraries written in C or C++. Of these warnings, most were associated with the Mesa graphics library and generated by interacting with graphical user interfaces. Merely logging into the GNOME desktop environment generated over 500 unique warnings. Of all warnings, the clear majority was about virtual table pointers. The associated stack traces were also lengthy in general. With these and other results, the paper contributes to the empirical literature on C and C++.

</details>


### [12] [Mind your key: An Empirical Study of LLM API Credential Leakage in iOS Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12212)
*Pinran Gao,Lingxiang Wang,Ying Zhang,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对iOS应用中LLM API密钥泄漏进行了大规模实证研究。通过对444个应用的动态分析，发现282个应用（63.5%）存在可被利用的凭据泄漏，包括JWT令牌泄漏（48%）、未认证后端代理访问（33%）和明文API密钥传输（19%）。在负责任披露三个月后，仅28%的应用完成了修复，72%仍存在可利用漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在移动应用中的快速集成引入了新的凭据安全风险——泄漏的凭据可被用于未经授权访问LLM推理服务，造成开发者的经济损失。此前关于凭据泄漏的研究主要集中在Android应用上，尚无实证研究系统性地调查iOS应用中LLM API密钥的泄漏情况。

Method: 构建了从1092个候选应用筛选出的444个iOS应用的高质量数据集；开发了LLMKeyLens动态分析框架，通过流量拦截、特定提供商的密钥提取和主动有效性验证来检测LLM API密钥泄漏，无需源代码访问或二进制解密。

Result: 282个应用在网络流量中暴露了可利用的LLM API凭据，涵盖至少十个提供商。识别出三种泄漏模式：JWT令牌泄漏（48%）、未认证后端代理访问（33%）和明文API密钥传输（19%）。三个月后复测显示仅28%修复，72%仍可利用。

Conclusion: LLM API密钥泄漏在iOS生态系统中既普遍又持久，暴露出开发者实践与安全集成原则之间的系统性鸿沟。安全的LLM集成不仅需要开发者意识提升，还需要来自提供商的明确安全指导和平台层面的强制执行机制。

Abstract: The rapid integration of large language models (LLMs) into mobile applications has introduced a new class of credential security risk: leaked credentials that grant unauthorized access to LLM inference services, causing financial damage to developers. Prior work on credential leakage has focused primarily on Android apps; to date, no empirical study has systematically investigated LLM API key leakage in iOS applications.
  We present the first in-depth empirical study of API key leakage in LLM-integrated apps. We construct a high-quality dataset of 444 iOS applications, filtered from 1092 candidates through a standardized process, and develop LLMKeyLens, a dynamic analysis framework that detects LLM API key leakage via traffic interception, provider-specific key extraction, and active validity confirmation, requiring neither source code access nor binary decryption. Our analysis reveals that 282 applications expose exploitable LLM API credentials in network traffic, spanning at least ten providers. We identify three leakage patterns: JWT-based token leakage (48%), unauthenticated backend proxy access (33%), and plaintext API key transmission (19%). To assess remediation, we re-analyzed the same 282 vulnerable applications three months after responsible disclosure; only 28% had remediated the reported vulnerability, while 72% remained exploitable, with persistent issues stemming from unauthenticated backends and broken JWT implementations.
  Our findings show that LLM API key leakage is both prevalent and persistent in the iOS ecosystem, exposing a systemic gap between developer practice and secure integration principles, and suggest that secure LLM integration requires not only developer awareness but also explicit security guidance from providers and platform-level enforcement.

</details>


### [13] [Rule Taxonomy and Evolution in AI IDEs: A Mining and Survey Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12231)
*Guangzong Cai,Ruiyin Li,Peng Liang,Zengyang Li,Mojtaba Shahin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法对AI IDE中的"规则"这一新型软件工件进行了实证研究，建立了包含5个一级和25个二级类别的分类体系，发现开发者优先级与实际配置之间存在差异，规则更新主要由建设性上下文扩展（29.17%）和丰富（26.59%）驱动，且规则更新能使工件合规率平均提升22.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI IDE中引入的"规则"作为新型软件工件，允许开发者将项目特定的约束和架构指南持久注入LLM上下文，但其分类体系、演化规律和实际影响尚未被系统探索。

Method: 采用混合方法实证研究：挖掘83个开源项目提取7,310条规则建立分类体系，结合99名从业者的调查问卷进行三角验证，分析1,540个规则演化事件，并对160个规则演化事件进行工件合规性评估。

Result: 1) 建立了包含5个一级和25个二级类别的规则分类体系；2) 开发者认为架构约束最重要，但实际仓库中主要是低层级工作流和代码格式约束；3) 规则更新主要由建设性上下文扩展（29.17%）和丰富（26.59%）驱动；4) 开发者自述修改规则主要为纠正AI错误（77.78%），通常通过添加新的负面约束而非编辑现有规则；5) 规则更新后工件平均合规率从49.14%提升至72.13%，提升22.99%。

Conclusion: 本研究为开发者优化提示策略提供了实证见解，并为工具开发者设计AI IDE的自动化冲突检测和上下文管理机制提供了指导。

Abstract: The adoption of AI-powered Integrated Development Environments (AI IDEs) has introduced "Rules" as a novel software artifact, allowing developers to persistently inject project-specific constraints and architectural guidelines into the context of Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite their role in aligning AI behavior with developer intent, the taxonomy, evolution, and practical impact of these rules remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we conducted a mixed-methods empirical study on AI IDE rules. By mining 83 open-source projects and extracting 7,310 rules, we established a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 5 primary and 25 secondary categories. We then triangulated these artifacts with survey responses from 99 practitioners. Our analysis identified a contrast between developer priorities and actual configurations: while practitioners rate architectural constraints as highly important, rule files in repositories primarily consist of low-level workflow and code formatting constraints. Furthermore, our analysis of 1,540 rule evolution events revealed that rules are updated frequently. Repository data further indicate that rule evolution is primarily driven by constructive context expansions (29.17%) and enrichments (26.59%). In contrast, surveyed developers reported modifying rules primarily to correct AI errors (77.78%), typically by adding new negative constraints rather than editing existing ones. Finally, an artifact compliance assessment of 160 rule evolution events revealed that updating rules significantly improves the adherence of software artifacts, with the average artifact compliance rate increasing by 22.99% (from 49.14% to 72.13%) following an update. Our study provides empirical insights that can help developers optimize prompting strategies and guide tool builders in designing automated conflict-detection and context-management mechanisms for AI IDEs.

</details>

### [14] [Towards a Joint Understanding of Remote Operation for Vehicles in Public Road Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11336)
*Elisabeth Shi,Maria-Magdalena Wolf,Nina Theobald,Bettina Abendroth,Eugen Wige,Johannes Springer,Katharina Hottelart,Andreas Schrank,Thorben Brandt,Michael Oehl,Frank Diermeyer,Lena Plum*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文论述了远程操作（遥操作）作为弥补当前驾驶自动化系统无法处理所有交通情境的关键技术，并提出了一个基于信息处理差异的术语框架，以促进不同学科和利益相关者之间的跨学科交流与合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前驾驶自动化系统尚无法处理人类驾驶员能应对的所有交通情境，因此无法直接实现完全无人驾驶的出行服务。远程操作被视为弥合这一差距的关键手段，但跨学科和跨利益相关者之间的沟通存在术语和理解上的障碍。

Method: 本文追溯术语至人类与车辆两侧信息处理的原始差异，构建了一个统一的术语框架，并将近期讨论的遥操作形式整合进该框架中。

Result: 提出了一个基于信息处理差异的术语框架，能够帮助不同背景的研究者和利益相关者建立共同理解，并成功整合了多种遥操作形式。

Conclusion: 该术语框架通过明确不同信息处理方式所需的规范，有助于推动跨学科对话，为安全实现远程操作在公共道路上的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Sustained driving automation systems are envisioned to be used as the foundation for driverless mobility services. However, both researchers and practitioners acknowledge that current driving automation systems are not yet able to handle all traffic situations that a human driver can handle. To bridge this gap and enable mobility services without an in-vehicle human driver or fallback, remote operation (or teleoperation) is increasingly discussed. Recently, first legal actions have been taken to enable some forms of remote operation on public roads. Remote operation encompasses a broad spectrum of methods to support a driving automation system, ranging from remote assistance, which includes providing information or releasing a maneuver, to remote driving, which includes driving the vehicle from a remote location. As such, safe implementation of remote operation in public road traffic challenges the collaboration of multiple academic disciplines (e.g. engineering, psychology, informatics, law, etc.) and stakeholders (e.g. remote operation service providers, remote operators, vehicle manufacturers, regulatory authorities, etc.). At the same time, the interdisciplinary discourse is often challenging due to differing expectations and language. To build a common ground, this article traces terminology back to the original differences in information processing both on human and vehicle side. This framework aims to help further discourse by directly specifying what is needed to engage a diverse audience including researchers and stakeholders of different backgrounds and interests. Recently discussed forms of teleoperation are integrated into this framework.

</details>


### [15] [3-Key-Input: Exploring the Theoretical Minimum Keys for Text Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11642)
*Naoki Kimura*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了使用2-5个物理键结合语言模型进行英文文本输入的效果，发现3个键配合GPT-4o即可达到9.46%的字符错误率和12.20%的词错误率，是通用英语输入的实用下限，增加键数带来的边际收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在辅助设备和移动设备等受限场景下，通过减少物理按键数量来增加硬件设计自由度，同时利用现代语言模型进行消歧，以确定最少需要多少个物理键才能实现可用的英文文本输入。

Method: 在300句英文语料（商务/会话/技术各100句）上，系统比较了按键数量（2-5）、字母到键的映射方式（基于布局/基于频率/故意最差情况）和解码器（Trie、GPT-2束搜索、GPT-4o选择）对输入性能的影响。

Result: 3键+GPT-4o达到CER 9.46%和WER 12.20%，比2键（CER 23.3%）降低59%的错误率；5键时CER降至5.4%但边际收益递减；不同映射方式影响很小（ΔCER < 0.5 pp）；技术类文本错误率约为商务类的两倍。

Conclusion: 在强语言模型先验的离线评估设置下，3个物理键是通用英文输入的实用最小值。

Abstract: How far can we reduce the number of physical keys if we endow an ambiguous keyboard with modern language models? Fewer keys increase hardware design freedom in constrained settings such as assistive devices and mobile form factors. This paper systematically evaluates text entry systems using 2-5 physical keys combined with language-model-based disambiguation. On a 300-sentence English corpus (100 sentences each for Business / Conversational / Technical), we compare key counts (2-5), letter-to-key mappings (layout-based / frequency-based / intentionally worst-case), and decoders (Trie-only, GPT-2 beam search, GPT-4o selection). We find that 3 keys + GPT-4o achieves character error rate (CER) 9.46% and word error rate (WER) 12.20%, reducing CER by 59% relative to 2 keys (CER 23.3%). At 3 keys, the key-stream entropy is 1.54 bits/char; while increasing to 5 keys improves accuracy (CER 5.4%), the marginal gains diminish. Mapping choice has a small impact under standard designs (ΔCER < 0.5 pp), and even an intentionally worst mapping degrades CER by only +0.5 pp, whereas Technical sentences yield roughly twice the error rate of Business. These results suggest that, in our evaluated offline setting under a strong LM prior, 3 keys are a practical minimum for general English.

</details>


### [16] [Learning by Chatting? Investigating the Impact of Generative AI on Information Seeking and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11669)
*Shravika Mittal,Su Lin Blodgett,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过8天的实地实验比较ChatGPT与Google搜索对非正式学习的影响，发现使用ChatGPT的参与者信息选择自主性降低、元认知负荷增加，且因AI输出偏向解决方案而非原理性知识、对话式交互减少探索，导致学习效果（尤其是高阶批判性学习）更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI工具在信息寻求任务中如何影响人类的学习与知识获取，尤其是在非正式学习情境下的认知后果。

Method: 采用组间实地实验设计，参与者在8天内通过ChatGPT或Google搜索进行非正式学习，并采用每日日记协议收集信息寻求过程的现场数据。

Result: ChatGPT组参与者在信息寻求过程中自主性降低，将大量信息选择工作外包给AI，由此产生的控制感减弱导致元认知负荷增加；ChatGPT输出偏向解决方案而非原理性知识，其对话式交互范式系统性地减少了用户对更广泛知识空间的探索；ChatGPT组平均学习效果（尤其是高阶批判性学习）差于Google组。

Conclusion: 将信息寻求外包给AI与有意义学习之间存在内在张力，该研究为理解AI对人类认知的风险提供了更广泛启示。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) tools offer increasing opportunities for augmenting human cognitive tasks. Among these tasks, information seeking is being rapidly reshaped by GenAI tools, with potentially profound implications for learning and knowledge acquisition. To investigate these implications, we conducted a between-subjects field experiment in which participants pursued informal learning by seeking information through either ChatGPT or Google Search over a span of 8 days. Using a daily diary protocol, we gathered in-situ data on their information-seeking processes. Our findings show that participants in the ChatGPT group experienced diminished agency in their information-seeking processes, as they offloaded much of the information selection to AI, and consequently experienced greater meta-cognitive load arising from this reduced sense of control. We further highlight two sources of distortion in information access when using ChatGPT: biases in ChatGPT outputs, particularly towards providing solution-oriented artifacts over principled knowledge; and systematic shifts in users' information-seeking behaviors, whereby the conversational and socially-oriented interaction paradigm of current GenAI tools may inadvertently reduce exploration of the broader knowledge space. As a result, on average, participants in the ChatGPT group had worse learning outcomes than those using Google, especially for higher-order critical learning. Our work suggests inherent tensions between offloading information seeking to AI and meaningful learning, and provides broader implications for understanding AI's risks to human cognition.

</details>


### [17] [Understanding and Supporting Online Discussion with Opinionated Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11693)
*Tianqi Song,Chi-Lan Yang,Zihan Liu,Zhengtao Xu,Yibin Feng,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验发现，与持反对意见的聊天机器人互动会促使参与者更大程度地改变观点，而与持赞同意见的机器人互动则会使参与者采用更友好的沟通方式，表明观点型聊天机器人能够影响个体的观点和在线交流行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观点型聊天机器人在在线平台上日益普及，但此前尚不清楚它们如何影响用户后续的在线互动和讨论行为。

Method: 设计控制实验，让83名参与者分别与表达反对、赞同或平衡观点的三类聊天机器人互动，随后测量其观点变化、沟通风格及信任感知。

Result: 持反对意见的机器人导致更大的观点转变；持赞同意见的机器人使参与者采用更随和的沟通风格；不同类型的机器人还导致不同程度的信任感及对机器人和人类对话者的不同感知。

Conclusion: 观点型聊天机器人既能影响个人对社会话题的观点，也能改变其在线沟通行为，设计者需在促进认知灵活性与维持积极用户体验及信任之间权衡。

Abstract: Opinionated chatbots are increasingly present on online platforms and have the potential to shape public discourse by influencing individuals' viewpoints before they engage in discussions. Despite their growing presence, the impact of interacting with opinionated chatbots on subsequent online interactions remains largely unexplored. This study investigated how exposure to different types of opinionated chatbots, specifically those expressing opposing, reinforcing, or balanced viewpoints, affected participants' subsequent online discussions. In a controlled experiment with 83 participants, we found that interacting with an opinionated chatbot that consistently opposed participants' arguments led to greater shifts in opinion, indicating enhanced openness to revising one's initial stance. Conversely, participants who interacted with a chatbot that consistently reinforced their views were more likely to adopt more agreeable communication styles in subsequent conversations with others. Furthermore, interactions with different types of opinionated chatbots resulted in varying levels of trust, as well as different perceptions of chatbots and human interlocutors. Our findings indicate that opinionated chatbots can influence both individuals' opinions on social topics and their communication behaviors in online environments. This presents a trade-off for future designers seeking to facilitate cognitive flexibility in changing opinions while maintaining positive user experiences and trust in the chatbots during public discourse. We discuss the implications for designing opinionated chatbots to promote more constructive and less polarized online

</details>


### [18] [Designing AI-Supported Focus Groups: A Role x Modality Playbook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11835)
*Zhiqing Wang,Steven Dow*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于AI如何支持焦点小组这一设计研究方法。论文梳理了AI在实时对话中的辅助能力（如提示、话题管理、实时总结），并将其映射为针对焦点小组的“剧本”，按AI角色（工具、联合主持人、主持人）和交互模态（文本、语音、具身）分类，同时讨论了交互权衡和开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦点小组是设计研究中收集参与者生活经验的核心方法，但资源密集且对主持技巧高度敏感。虽然生成式AI已在实时对话辅助方面展现出潜力，但用户体验研究（UXR）团队缺乏清晰的认知框架来理解这些能力在焦点小组中的具体应用场景及其方法论风险。

Method: 论文通过综合梳理现有AI支持实时对话的相关工作，将其翻译并映射到焦点小组的具体情境中，构建了一个按AI角色（工具、联合主持人、主持人）和交互模态（文本、语音、具身）组织的AI支持剧本（playbook）。

Result: 提出了一个面向焦点小组的AI支持分类框架（剧本），刻画了不同AI角色和模态下的交互权衡，并识别了评估AI支持的焦点小组作为方法论配置时面临的开放问题。

Conclusion: AI可以以不同角色和模态介入焦点小组，但每种配置都带来方法论的权衡与风险，需要进一步研究评估其有效性及对研究质量的影响。

Abstract: Collecting participants' lived experiences is central to design research. Focus groups are uniquely valuable because participants not only share individual accounts but also respond to one another, surfacing comparison, disagreement, and collective sensemaking. However, focus groups are resource-intensive and highly sensitive to facilitation: moderators must probe for specificity, balance participation, manage topic flow, and sustain psychological safety, and subtle facilitation choices can shape what becomes salient. Recent HCI work and commercial meeting tools show that generative AI can scaffold live conversation through prompting, turn regulation, thematic mapping, and real-time summarization. Yet UXR teams lack a clear map of what these capabilities mean in focus groups and what methodological risks they introduce. We synthesize AI supports for live conversation and translate them into a focus-group-specific playbook organized by AI role (tool, co-host, host) and modality (text, voice, embodied).We synthesize prior work on AI-supported live conversation and propose a focus-group-specific playbook of AI supports organized by role (tool, co-host, host) and modality (text, voice, embodied). We characterize interactional trade-offs and identify open questions for evaluating AI-supported focus groups as methodological configurations.

</details>


### [19] [PAPEL: A Collaborative System for Parental Guidance during Preschool Play-Based English Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11896)
*Xutong Wang,Yu Mei,Qinwei Li,Muyu Liu,Xiwen Yao,Chang Liu,Zhoutong Ye,Jie Cai,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAPEL系统，一个家长-AI协作系统，通过内容生成、语言适应、平衡评估和扩展响应四个模块，帮助家长在开放式游戏中将英语作为外语融入亲子互动。研究发现，相比轻量级聊天机器人基线，PAPEL能促进更多整合了游戏与教学元素的家长话语以及更多亲子对话轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家长在家庭环境中难以将开放式游戏转化为有效的英语作为外语（EFL）学习体验，面临内容选择、语言表达、教学与游戏平衡以及问题解决等关键挑战。

Method: 通过访谈和Wizard-of-Oz研究进行形成性研究，识别四个关键挑战；提出PAPEL系统，包含内容生成、语言适应、平衡评估和扩展响应四个模块；采用16对亲子对的平衡被试内设计进行实验评估。

Result: 与轻量级聊天机器人基线相比，PAPEL系统与更多整合了游戏和教学内容的家长话语以及更多亲子对话轮次相关。

Conclusion: 基于游戏场景的家长-AI协作系统能有效支持家长在开放式游戏中融入外语学习，增强亲子互动质量。

Abstract: Play-based parent-child interaction offers preschoolers rich opportunities for everyday foreign language learning, yet many parents struggle to turn open-ended play into effective English-as-a-Foreign-Language (EFL) learning experiences at home. To explore how AI might support this process, we conducted formative studies through interviews and a Wizard-of-Oz study. We identified four key challenges: content selection, language expression, balancing instruction and play, and problem solving. To address these challenges, we present PAPEL, a parent-AI collaborative system that grounds suggestions in the ongoing play scene and organizes support into four core modules: content generation, language adaptation, balance assessment, and extended response. In a counterbalanced within-subjects study with 16 parent-child dyads, PAPEL was associated with more integrated parent utterances that combined playful and instructional content, as well as more parent-child conversational turns, than the lightweight chatbot baseline used in our study.

</details>


### [20] [Frozen Multimodal Embeddings for Personality and Cognitive Ability Assessment in Asynchronous Video Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11930)
*Kuo-En Hung,Hung-Yue Suen,Shih-Ching Yeh,Hsiang-Wen Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对ACM多媒体AVI挑战赛2026，提出了一种基于冻结多模态编码器（CLIP、Whisper、RoBERTa等）的小样本表示学习方法。在Track-1人格预测任务中，通过特质特异性回归和晚期融合系统实现了0.2696的验证MSE，相比基线降低19.1%；在Track-2认知能力分类任务中，发现数据集存在主体属性捷径，而非稳健的认知推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从异步视频面试中预测心理特质是一个具有挑战性的多模态学习问题，因为标注数据集有限，而每个回答包含高维的视觉、声学和言语信号。

Method: 将问题视为小样本表示学习任务，使用冻结的多模态编码器（CLIP提取视觉特征、Whisper提取声学特征和转录文本、RoBERTa/E5/DeBERTaV3提取文本表示），后接低容量下游模型。Track-1采用特质特异性回归和晚期融合策略。

Result: Track-1：验证MSE为0.2696，相比官方基线0.3334降低19.1%；消融实验显示从全局模型(0.3189)到每特质建模(0.2871)到每特质晚期融合(0.2696)的三步提升。Track-2：主体属性基线达0.5781准确率，多模态集成达0.5313，均高于官方基线0.4062。

Conclusion: 基于AVI的心理评估受益于特质特异性多模态建模，但认知能力预测需要仔细控制数据集中的捷径偏差。

Abstract: Predicting psychological traits from asynchronous video interviews (AVIs) is a challenging multimodal learning problem because labeled datasets are limited while each response contains high-dimensional visual, acoustic, and verbal signals. This paper presents our solution for the ACM Multimedia AVI Challenge 2026, which evaluates two tasks: Track~1 predicts self-reported HEXACO personality traits from personality-related interview responses, and Track~2 classifies cognitive ability levels from structured AVI responses. We treat the problem as a small-sample representation learning task. Instead of fine-tuning large pretrained models, we use frozen multimodal encoders, including CLIP for visual features, Whisper for acoustic features and transcripts, and RoBERTa, E5, and DeBERTaV3 for textual representations, followed by low-capacity downstream models. For Track~1, our trait-specific regression and late-fusion system achieves an average validation MSE of 0.2696, improving over the official baseline of 0.3334. Ablation results show a three-step improvement from a global model (0.3189), to per-trait modeling (0.2871), to per-trait late fusion (0.2696), corresponding to a 19.1\% relative MSE reduction over the official baseline. For Track~2, a compact subject-attribute baseline reaches 0.5781 accuracy, while our multimodal ensemble reaches 0.5313, both above the official baseline of 0.4062. We interpret this result as evidence of possible subject-attribute shortcuts in the validation split rather than robust cognitive inference from AVI content. Overall, our findings suggest that AVI-based psychological assessment benefits from trait-specific multimodal modeling, but cognitive ability prediction requires careful control of dataset shortcuts.

</details>


### [21] [Somewhere Over the Desktop: A Research Agenda for Ubiquitous Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11980)
*Niklas Elmqvist,Panagiotis D. Ritsos,Peter W. S. Butcher*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 空间计算、生成式AI与开放网络标准正在融合，三大空间操作系统（Android XR、Meta Horizon OS、Apple visionOS）已具备平台级场景理解能力。这种融合为无处不在的分析（UA）创造了新机遇，但专有平台的设计惯例正在固化。本文梳理了UA的知识谱系，将其组织为认知、上下文、交互、平台、可视化、协作和评估七个聚类，并交叉生成42个未来研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间计算、生成式AI与开放网络标准正在融合，三大空间操作系统已具备平台级场景理解能力。然而，专有平台正在固化设计惯例，缺乏基于证据的替代方案。UA领域已成熟到可以系统梳理其知识谱系，以指导未来发展。

Method: 通过谱系学方法，系统梳理UA的知识历史，将其组织为七个聚类（认知、上下文、交互、平台、可视化、协作、评估），并通过交叉聚类生成42个未来研究挑战。

Result: 形成了UA的结构化谱系，涵盖七个核心聚类，并交叉生成了42个未来研究挑战。

Conclusion: 在空间计算与AI融合的背景下，UA面临新的机遇与挑战。需要有基于证据的替代方案来应对专有平台的设计固化。42个交叉研究挑战为UA的未来发展提供了系统化方向。

Abstract: Spatial computing, generative AI, and open web standards are converging. Three spatial operating systems -- Android XR, Meta Horizon OS, and Apple visionOS -- now ship with platform-level scene understanding. Wearable displays span the range from full headsets to slim smartglasses. Agentic AI operates on the same spatial substrates as the human user. This convergence enables new opportunities for \textit{ubiquitous analytics} (UA): the use of many, physically distributed, networked devices to support data sensemaking anytime and anywhere. But proprietary platforms are settling design conventions that will calcify without evidence-based alternatives. UA has now matured to the point where its intellectual history can be read as a structured genealogy of foundations, contributions, and lineages. We trace this genealogy and organize it into clusters spanning cognition, context, interaction, platforms, visualization, collaboration, and evaluation. Finally, we cross these clusters with each other, yielding a total of 42 future research challenges.

</details>


### [22] [Channels and Substrates: Distributed Cognition as an Interaction Model for Ubiquitous Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11986)
*Niklas Elmqvist,Panagiotis D. Ritsos,Peter W. S. Butcher*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布式认知的交互模型，用于泛在分析（UA）和跨设备（XVA）环境。该模型将交互视为表征状态在多种基质（如心智、语言、身体、人工制品和设备）间的传播，而非单一界面的信息流。在此基础上，作者引入了输入通道和输出通道的概念，作为对数据可视化中视觉通道的泛化，通过重分析多个泛在、沉浸式和情境分析系统验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人机交互模型假设单一整体式界面和稳定的感知-运动环路，这种假设在跨设备和泛在分析场景中表现不佳——交互式数据分析往往跨越多台设备、多种人工制品和多个人员，在从办公室到工厂车间等不同环境中展开。因此需要一种新的交互模型来更好地描述和理解这种复杂场景。

Method: 将分布式认知理论引入交互建模，将交互重新定义为表征状态在多种基质（心智、语言、身体、人工制品、设备）之间的传播。在此基础上，将可视化中的视觉通道泛化为输入通道和输出通道，这些通道通过基质的属性承载表征状态，其可用性、适宜性和偏好性取决于具体情境。通过重分析多个泛在、沉浸式和情境分析系统来展示该框架。

Result: 提出了通道与基质框架（channels and substrates framework），能够更准确地描述跨设备和泛在分析中的交互行为。通过多个案例的重分析展示了该框架的适用性和解释力。

Conclusion: 泛在分析中的交互应被建模为表征状态在多种基质间的传播，而非通过单一界面的信息流；输入/输出通道作为视觉通道的泛化，结合基质的可用性、适宜性和偏好性，为理解和设计跨设备交互提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Traditional HCI interaction models assume a single monolithic interface and a stable sensorimotor loop. These models fit poorly with cross-device (XVA) and ubiquitous analytics (UA), where interactive data sensemaking unfolds across multiple devices, artifacts, and people in disparate settings from the office to the factory floor. In this paper, we show how interaction in ubiquitous analytics can be modeled using distributed cognition as propagation of representational state across substrates -- minds, speech, bodies, artifacts, and devices -- rather than as traffic through a single interface. On this basis we introduce input and output channels as generalizations of the visual channels from data visualization: just as visual channels carry data through properties of the visual substrate, input and output channels carry representational state through substrates whose availability, suitability, and preferability depend on context. We demonstrate the channels and substrates framework by reanalyzing several ubiquitous, immersive, and situated analytics systems.

</details>


### [23] [Identifying cybersickness causes in virtual reality games using symbolic machine learning algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12214)
*Thiago Porcino,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Flavia Bernardini,Daniela Trevisan,Esteban Clua*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文使用符号机器学习方法，基于37名有效志愿者的实验数据，分析了VR游戏中导致晕动症（CS）的各种因素，发现旋转和加速度在飞行游戏中比赛车游戏更容易引发不适，且VR经验不足的受试者更易感到不适，短期与长期暴露下触发不适的原因也不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR和头戴显示器在多个领域越来越普及，但伴随的晕动症（CS）问题严重影响用户体验，现有研究对其诱因的量化分析不足，因此亟需系统性地识别和排序导致CS的关键因素。

Method: 采用符号机器学习方法，结合经典机器学习算法，设计了两款VR游戏（飞行游戏和赛车游戏）及6种实验协议，对88名志愿者（有效样本37个）进行实验，通过特征重要性排序分析CS诱因。

Result: 旋转和加速度在飞行游戏中比赛车游戏更易引发CS；VR经验较少的受试者更易感到不适；先前经验在赛车游戏中影响更大（因该游戏提供更多控制器自由度、位移选择和用户控制的加速度）；短期与长期VR暴露下触发不适的原因存在差异。

Conclusion: CS的诱因因游戏类型和暴露时长而异，建议针对短期和长期暴露场景分别制定缓解策略，并对比了赛车与飞行两种典型场景的差异。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) and head-mounted displays are constantly gaining popularity in various fields such as education, military, entertainment, and health. Although such technologies provide a high sense of immersion, they can also trigger symptoms of discomfort. This condition is called cybersickness (CS) and is quite popular in recent virtual reality publications. This work proposes a novel experimental analysis using symbolic machine learning to rank potential causes of CS in VR games. We estimate CS causes and rank them according to their impact using classical machine learning. Experiments are performed using two virtual reality games and 6 experimental protocols along with 37 valid samples from a total of 88 volunteers. Our results show that rotation and acceleration triggered cybersickness more frequently in a flight game in contrast to a race game. We could also observe that subjects that are less experienced with VR are more prone to feel discomfort. Former experience plays a more important role on the race game, as this game provides more liberty to the user in terms of controllers, more displacement alternatives and a more user-controlled acceleration. Furthermore, different causes that trigger discomfort arise based on short or long term VR exposures. We suggest strategies for mitigating CS for these two scenarios: short and long term exposure experiences and compare the two highlighted scenarios (race and flight).

</details>

### [24] [Quasi-linear Time Multiplication of Sparse Polynomials with Integer Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12100)
*Qiao-Long Huang,Yichuan Cao,Ruichen Qiu,Xiao-Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏多项式乘法这一开放问题，指出了先前声称的拟线性时间输出敏感算法的错误，并提出了在整数系数和有限域系数下的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏多项式乘法是计算机代数与计算理论中的基础问题，发展拟线性时间的输出敏感乘法算法一直是一个公开挑战。

Method: 利用现有的拟线性模-黑盒插值算法，针对整数系数设计了拟线性比特复杂度的算法；对于有限域系数，设计了比特复杂度与项数、度对数及有限域大小对数成线性关系的算法。

Result: 整数系数下实现了拟线性比特复杂度；有限域系数下实现了线性比特复杂度（关于项数、度对数、域大小对数）。

Conclusion: 纠正了此前声称的拟线性时间输出敏感乘法算法的错误，并为不同系数域提供了相应的高效稀疏多项式乘法算法。

Abstract: Sparse polynomial multiplication is a fundamental problem in computer algebra and the theory of computation, and the development of a quasi-linear time output-sensitive multiplication algorithm has been posed as an open challenge. In this paper, a counterexample is provided to a previously claimed solution to this open problem for integer coefficients. By employing the existing quasi-linear modular-black-box interpolation algorithm, we are able to provide an algorithm with quasi-linear bit complexity for the integer coefficients setting. Furthermore, in the case of coefficients over a finite field, we obtain an algorithm whose bit complexity is linear in the number of terms, the logarithm of the degree, and the logarithm of the size of the finite field.

</details>


### [25] [Sparse Polynomial Divisibility Test over Finite Field is CoNP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12130)
*Yichuan Cao,Ruichen Qiu,Qiao-Long Huang,Ruyong Feng,Xiao-Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限域上判断一个稀疏多项式是否不能整除另一个稀疏多项式是NP难的（在BPP归约下），即稀疏多项式可整除性测试是CoNP难的，解决了有限域上稀疏多项式整除性测试计算复杂度的长期开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限域上稀疏多项式整除性测试计算复杂度的长期开放问题。

Method: 通过BPP多一归约，将稀疏多项式不可整除性判定问题归约到已知的NP难问题，从而证明其NP-hardness，并等价得出可整除性测试的CoNP-hardness。

Result: 证明了有限域上稀疏多项式不可整除性判定是NP-hard（在BPP归约下），稀疏多项式可整除性测试是CoNP-hard。

Conclusion: 有限域上稀疏多项式整除性测试问题的计算复杂度得到了确定，即CoNP-hard。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that deciding whether a sparse polynomial does not divide another sparse polynomial exactly over finite fields is NP-hard under BPP many-one reductions. Equivalently, the sparse polynomial divisibility test over finite fields is CoNP-hard. This resolves the long-standing open problem concerning the computational complexity of the divisibility test for sparse polynomials in the setting of finite fields.

</details>


### [26] [Output-sensitive Sparse Polynomial GCD over Finite Fields is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12144)
*Ruichen Qiu,Yichuan Cao,Qiao-Long Huang,Ruyong Feng,Xiao-Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限域上，输出敏感的多项式GCD计算在BPP多一归约下是NP难的，即不存在多项式时间（关于输入和输出大小）的随机化算法来计算两个稀疏单变元多项式的最大公因式，除非NP ⊆ BPP。同时证明了有限域上单位根检测问题（判断稀疏多项式与x^n-1的GCD是否有非零度）也是NP难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决The Sparsity Challenges中提出的Challenge 5在有限域上的开放问题，即确定输出敏感的稀疏多项式GCD计算的计算复杂度。

Method: 采用BPP多一归约，将已知的NP难问题归约到输出敏感的稀疏多项式GCD计算问题，以及有限域上的单位根检测问题。

Result: 1) 输出敏感的稀疏单变元多项式GCD计算在有限域上是NP难的（在标准复杂度假设NP ⊄ BPP下）；2) 有限域上的单位根检测问题也是NP难的。

Conclusion: 在有限域上，不可能存在关于输入和输出大小多项式时间的随机化算法来计算稀疏多项式的最大公因式（除非NP ⊆ BPP），从而回答了该领域的开放问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that output-sensitive sparse polynomial GCD computation over finite fields is NP-hard under BPP many-one reduction. More precisely, for two sparse univariate polynomials $f,g$ with finite field coefficients, there exists no randomized algorithm to compute $\mathrm{gcd}(f,g)$, which is polynomial-time in the sizes of $f,g,\gcd(f,g)$ under the standard complexity assumption $\mathrm{NP}\nsubseteq\mathrm{BPP}$. This settles the open problem posed as Challenge 5 in The Sparsity Challenges in the finite field setting. Furthermore, we show that the Roots of Unity Detection problem over finite fields is NP-hard; that is, determining whether the GCD of a sparse univariate polynomial and $x^n - 1$ has nonzero degree is NP-hard.

</details>

### [27] [LLMs+Graphs: Toward Graph-Native, Synergistic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11560)
*Arijit Khan,Longxu Sun,Xin Huang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型（LLMs）与图计算在三个协同方向上的融合：LLMs结合图计算进行检索与推理、LLMs与知识图谱的双向集成、以及图算法增强的AI智能体；同时探讨了LLMs在图数据管理和图机器学习中的新能力，为构建下一代图原生AI系统提供了统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在结构化与多跳推理中存在局限性，而图结构数据支撑着多个关键领域的应用，因此需要探究如何将LLMs与图计算相结合，实现更接地气、上下文丰富的推理能力。

Method: 通过综述三种互补的协同方向（LLMs+图计算检索推理、LLMs+知识图谱双向集成、图算法增强的AI智能体），以及LLMs在图数据管理与图机器学习中的新应用，整合算法、系统与设计原则。

Result: 提出了集成LLMs、图数据管理、图挖掘、图机器学习与智能体计算的下一代图原生AI系统的统一视角。

Conclusion: 为数据科学和数据挖掘研究人员提供了如何将LLMs与图计算各领域融合以构建图原生AI系统的系统性指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced rapidly, but their limitations in structured and multi-hop reasoning underscore the need for graph-native, synergistic artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Graph-structured data underpins critical applications across social, biological, financial, transportation, web, and knowledge domains, making it essential to understand how LLMs can leverage graph computation for grounded, context-rich inference. Three complementary synergies are emerging: LLMs augmented with graph computation for retrieval and reasoning; bidirectional integration between LLMs and knowledge graphs (KGs), where LLMs support KG construction and curation while KGs enforce semantic constraints and factual consistency; and AI agents strengthened by graph algorithms for planning, decision making, and multi-step reasoning. In parallel, LLMs introduce new capabilities for graph data management and graph machine learning (ML) through natural language interfaces and hybrid LLM-graph neural network (GNN) pipelines. This tutorial synthesizes the algorithms, systems, and design principles driving these converging directions, offering data science and data mining researchers a unified perspective on integrating LLMs, graph data management, graph mining, graph ML, and agentic computation into next-generation graph-native AI systems.

</details>


### [28] [Querying Cohesive Subgraph regarding Span-Constrained Triangles on Temporal Graphs with Dynamic Index Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11582)
*Chuhan Hu,Ming Zhong,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出面向时序图的(k,δ)-truss结构，要求三角形在短时间内存在，并设计了索引无关和索引基的查询方法。通过无损压缩所有(k,δ)-truss为图或树结构，实现空间大幅压缩和最优时间检索，索引压缩比可达10^{-4}，索引基方法比索引无关方法快2~4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图研究重新定义了静态图中的三角、模体、k-核等概念，受此启发，本文旨在将静态图中的k-truss概念拓展到时序图场景，以同时捕捉图结构在静态和时序两个维度上的内聚性。

Method: 提出(k,δ)-truss定义（要求三角形在时间窗口δ内存在）；设计了索引无关和索引基两类查询方法；利用(k,δ)-truss的双重包含关系，将其无损压缩为map或树结构；分别基于truss分解和truss维护两种方式构建索引；提出了索引的动态维护技术。

Result: 索引基方法处理查询达到交互时间级别，比索引无关方法快2~4个数量级；索引压缩比高达10^{-4}；索引可高效动态更新而无需从头重建。

Conclusion: 提出的(k,δ)-truss及其索引方案能有效支持时序图上的内聚子图查询，在查询效率、存储压缩和动态维护方面均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Recent advances in temporal graph research have redefined traditional static graph concepts such as triangles, motifs, and $k$-cores. Inspired by this, we introduce a novel $(k,δ)$-truss for temporal graphs, requiring triangles to exist within sufficiently short time windows. The $(k,δ)$-truss ensures both static and temporal cohesion, while the original $k$-truss is a special case when $δ= \infty$. To address $(k,δ)$-truss queries, we propose index-free and index-based approaches. Utilizing the dual containment relation of $(k,δ)$-trusses, our indexes losslessly compress all $(k,δ)$-trusses into map or tree structures, significantly reducing space while enabling optimal-time retrieval. To scale to large temporal graphs, we develop two index construction algorithms based on truss decomposition and truss maintenance, respectively, which substantially reduce redundant computations. Moreover, we present techniques for the dynamic maintenance of the proposed indexes. The experimental results demonstrate that index-based approaches process queries in interactive time and outperform the index-free approach by 2$\sim$4 orders of magnitude, while the indexes achieve compression ratios of up to $10^{-4}$ and can be updated efficiently without rebuilding from scratch.

</details>


### [29] [Efficient Graph Indexing for Interval-Aware Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11789)
*Siyuan Liang,Ziqi Yin,Qi Zhang,Ronghua Li,Guoren Wang,Kaiwen Xue,Daiyin Wang,Xubin Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出统一区间感知相对邻近图（URNG），一种支持多种区间感知ANN查询语义的统一图框架，并基于此开发了实用的图索引UG，在多个数据集上取得了优异的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区间感知近似最近邻搜索方法通常针对单一查询语义设计（如区间过滤ANN搜索），需要多种专用索引来支持不同工作负载，导致索引和内存开销过大。本文旨在解决这一局限性，提出统一的图框架以支持多种区间感知查询语义。

Method: 提出URNG统一图框架，在保持相对邻近图ANN索引的单调可搜索性的同时，确保查询诱导子图的结构继承性，使单一索引支持多种区间感知查询语义。在此基础上开发UG实用图索引，通过统一区间感知剪枝和迭代修复高效近似URNG，并设计相应的查询算法。

Result: 在5个数据集上的大量实验表明，UG在各种区间感知工作负载下持续实现优异的精度-效率权衡，同时保持有竞争力的索引构建成本和内存使用。

Conclusion: URNG和UG为区间感知ANN搜索提供了统一且高效的解决方案，克服了传统方法需多种专用索引的局限，在精度、效率、索引构建成本和内存使用之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Interval-aware Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search arises in applications where each object is associated with a numeric value or interval, and queries must satisfy both vector-similarity and interval constraints. Existing methods are typically tailored to a single query semantics, such as interval-filtered ANN search, and therefore require multiple specialized indexes to support diverse workloads, leading to substantial indexing and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose the Unified Interval-aware Relative Neighborhood Graph (URNG), a unified graph framework for interval-aware ANN search. URNG preserves the monotonic searchability of relative-neighborhood-graph based ANN indexes while additionally ensuring structural heredity over query-induced subgraphs, enabling a single index to support multiple interval-aware query semantics. Building on this framework, we develop UG, a practical graph index that efficiently approximates URNG through unified interval-aware pruning and iterative repair, together with a query algorithm for interval-aware ANN search. Extensive experiments on 5 datasets show that UG consistently achieves a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off across diverse interval-aware workloads while maintaining competitive index construction cost and memory usage.

</details>


### [30] [Neuro-Relational Programs: Unifying Queries and Neural Computation over Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11946)
*Arie Soeteman,Balder ten Cate,Maurice Funk,Benny Kimelfeld,Carsten Lutz,Moritz Schönherr*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出神经关系程序（NRPs），一种扩展Datalog风格的声明式查询语言，它将关系推理与可学习的神经组件（如向量嵌入的组合、聚合和变换）交织在一起，可同时被解读为带可训练组件的查询计划或内建关系结构的神经架构。NRP的不同语法片段能恢复现有架构（如GNN）和查询形式，其表达能力通过FOCQ逻辑与均匀TC⁰电路建立精确联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法处理关系数据库通常需要先将数据库转换为图表示（如GNN），或通过扩展查询机制联合处理嵌入和关系内容。作者希望设计一种统一的声明式形式，既能表达关系推理，又能融入可学习的神经组件，避免中间图转换的间接性。

Method: NRPs扩展了Datalog风格的规则，增加了对向量嵌入进行组合（combine）、聚合（aggregate）和变换（transform）的操作。通过定义零元、一元、前沿守卫等语法片段，分别对应非自适应查询算法、GNN消息传递/深度同态网络以及更一般的结构。利用FOCQ（带计数的一阶逻辑扩展）在实值结构上刻画无限制NRP（使用ReLU全连接变换）的表达能力，并建立与均匀TC⁰电路的联系。

Result: 零元NRP对应非自适应查询算法；一元NRP泛化GNN风格的消息传递并精确捕获深度同态网络；前沿守卫NRP覆盖带行ID的数据库。无限制NRP的表达能力等价于FOCQ，并在有序数据库上对应均匀TC⁰电路。

Conclusion: NRPs为关系数据上的查询和神经计算提供了一个广泛的声明式框架，统一了多种现有架构和查询形式，并通过逻辑和电路复杂度建立了清晰的理论表达力边界。

Abstract: The conventional approach to deep learning over relational databases applies neural models, such as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), to a graph representation of the database. Recent approaches instead operate on databases directly, associating tuples with embeddings and extending query mechanisms to jointly process embeddings and relational content. Inspired by these developments, we introduce Neuro-Relational Programs (NRPs), a declarative query language for relational databases whose facts carry numeric vector embeddings. NRPs extend Datalog-style rules with operations that combine, aggregate, and transform embeddings, thereby interleaving relational reasoning and learnable neural components within a single formalism. This yields a general approach to neural computation over relational data: an NRP can be read both as a query plan with trainable components and as a neural architecture with relational structure built in.
  Natural syntactic fragments of NRPs recover existing architectures and query formalisms. Zero-ary NRPs correspond to non-adaptive query algorithms; monadic NRPs generalize GNN-style message passing and precisely capture Deep Homomorphism Networks, a connection that we extend to frontier-guarded NRPs over databases with row-ids. We characterize the expressive power of unrestricted NRPs with ReLU-FFN transformations by FOCQ, an extension of first-order logic with counting interpreted over real-weighted structures, yielding a precise connection with uniform TC$^0$ over ordered databases. Together, these results establish NRPs as a broad declarative framework for querying and neural computation over relational data.

</details>


### [31] [TAHOE: Text-to-SQL with Automated Hint Optimization from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12387)
*Zhiyi Chen,Jie Song,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Tahoe是一个将提示优化视为动态数据管理问题的Text-to-SQL系统，通过错误驱动的提示学习管道在开发与部署阶段将调试轨迹整合到结构化提示库中，包含语法提示和语义提示，并引入策略层处理冲突的用户意图。在Spider 2.0-Snow基准上，使用GPT-5.5将通过率从61.95%提升至79.42%，无需更新模型参数，且提示库可迁移至更弱的骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在Text-to-SQL中从原型转向生产部署面临严格SQL方言、大规模模式、演化用户偏好等挑战，监督微调成本高且僵化，智能体测试时扩展昂贵，亟需一种轻量、动态且可部署的解决方案。

Method: Tahoe包括：(1) 错误驱动的提示学习管道，将编译器反馈提炼为语法提示，执行和用户反馈转化为语义提示；(2) 策略层，对冲突用户意图建模为竞争策略，利用时效信号和后学习归因统计；(3) 推理时，检索相关提示并通过逻辑规划+SQL合成引导LLM。

Result: 在Spider 2.0-Snow上，使用GPT-5.5将pass率从61.95%提升至79.42%，pass@4从72.57%提升至87.61%，Snowflake语法通过率100%，编译器反馈批评轮次从2.79降至0.12。提示库迁移至Doubao-2.0-lite带来19.7个百分点的pass率提升。

Conclusion: Tahoe通过将提示优化重构为数据管理问题，在不更新模型参数的情况下显著提升Text-to-SQL性能，具有跨模型迁移能力，为生产级Text-to-SQL部署提供了实用的轻量级方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have democratized database access through Text-to-SQL, but moving from prototypes to production remains difficult. Real deployments must handle strict SQL dialects, massive schemas, and evolving user preferences, while supervised fine-tuning is costly and rigid and agentic test-time scaling is expensive. We present Tahoe, a system that treats prompt optimization as a dynamic data management problem. Tahoe uses an error-driven hint learning pipeline across Development and Deployment to consolidate debugging traces into a structured Hint Bank. Compiler feedback is distilled into reusable Syntax Hints for dialect-specific rules, while execution and user feedback are converted into Semantic Hints for schema- and user-specific logic. Tahoe further introduces a Strategy Layer that models conflicting user intents as competing strategies under shared natural-language triggers, with recency signals and post-learning attribution statistics that summarize empirical success, harm, inertness, and support. At inference time, Tahoe retrieves relevant hints and guides the LLM through Logic Planning followed by SQL Synthesis. We implement and evaluate the development-phase workflow, leaving deployment-time human-feedback updates for future work. On Spider 2.0-Snow, Tahoe substantially improves Text-to-SQL without updating model parameters. On 113 supervised Spider 2.0-Snow-0212 examples using GPT-5.5, Tahoe raises pass rate from 61.95 percent to 79.42 percent and pass-at-4 from 72.57 percent to 87.61 percent, achieves 100 percent Snowflake syntax pass rate, and reduces average compiler-feedback critic rounds from 2.79 to 0.12 per sampled candidate. The same Hint Bank also transfers to weaker backbones, including a 19.7 percentage-point pass-rate gain on Doubao-2.0-lite.

</details>

### [32] [A Zero-Shot Multi-Agent Framework for Human-Building Interaction via Programmatic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11354)
*Yuqi Wang,Gulai Shen,Ali Mehmani*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化多智能体框架，利用语义路由和程序化推理将自然语言理解与建筑分析解耦，通过"门卫"机制进行任务分解，并采用专用编码智能体生成可执行Python脚本以实现精确计算，在200多栋商业建筑的数据集上验证了该方法在提供准确且具有上下文相关响应方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在人-建筑交互领域面临三大挑战：领域数据有限导致传统微调不切实际；LLM训练数据不透明需要谨慎集成领域知识以确保可靠性；不同LLM对齐特性差异大，需要多智能体方法才能同时实现自然交互和技术准确性。

Method: 采用层次化多智能体框架，包含语义路由和程序化推理两个核心机制。引入"门卫"(Doorman)机制进行任务分解，将自然语言理解与建筑分析解耦；使用专用编码智能体生成可执行的Python脚本进行精确算术计算，而非标准的RAG方法。

Result: 在来自200多栋商业建筑的数据集上验证了该框架的有效性，能够为不同用户（从租户到建筑管理者）在各种建筑系统应用中提供准确且具有上下文相关性的响应。

Conclusion: 该层次化多智能体框架成功解决了LLM在领域特定任务（如HBI）中面临的数据有限、可靠性要求高和多模型对齐等挑战，为LLM在专业领域的适应性提供了一种创新方法，在保持准确性和用户参与度的同时实现了自然交互。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) offers opportunities to enhance Human-Building Interaction (HBI) by enabling more direct interactions through intuitive interfaces to complex building systems. These systems can be characterized by the vast amounts of data across multiple formats, the lack of nonconfidential and generalizable information, and the requirement of domain expertise for interpretation. Applying LLMs to domain-specific tasks like HBI presents additional challenges. Limited training data makes traditional fine-tuning approaches impractical. Meanwhile, the opacity of LLM training data requires careful integration of domain knowledge to ensure reliability. Additionally, different LLMs exhibit varying alignment characteristics, suggesting that achieving both natural interaction and technical accuracy requires a multi-agent approach. These challenges highlight the need for innovative approaches to adapt LLMs for specialized domains while maintaining accuracy and user engagement. In this paper, we develop a hierarchical multi-agent framework that utilizes semantic routing and programmatic reasoning to decouple natural language understanding from building analytics. Instead of standard RAG approaches, our system employs a "Doorman" mechanism for task decomposition and specialized coding agents that generate executable Python scripts for precise arithmetic. We validate this framework on a dataset from more than 200 commercial buildings. Results demonstrate the effectiveness in providing accurate and contextual responses for diverse users, including stakeholders, from tenants to building managers, across various building system applications.

</details>

### [33] [MASK: Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling for Risk-Sensitive 6G Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11249)
*Ahmet Gunhan Aydin,Elif Tugce Ceran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MASK架构，通过语义重要性调度（A-SIG机制）仅允许top-K智能体在有限频谱资源下传输，实现与无通信约束基线相当的性能，并验证了对数据包擦除的鲁棒性，为6G资源受限系统提供关键解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G连接机器人需要在有限物理频谱资源下实现高性能协同控制，而现有方法在严格瞬时带宽约束下无法支持所有智能体同时传输，亟需一种资源高效且风险感知的调度机制。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK)架构，包含Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG)机制：基于局部语义重要性分数仅调度top-K智能体；通过自监督全局编码器将优先观测聚合为紧凑隐状态；采用分布策略缓解数据稀疏下的尾部风险。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，即使信道接入限制为群规模的一小部分，MASK仍能达到与无通信约束基线匹配的性能，并对数据包擦除具有内在鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义调度是资源受限6G系统的关键使能技术，有效协调了高性能控制与无线频谱约束之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Realizing the vision of 6G connected robotics requires reconciling high-performance collaborative control with the rigid spectral limitations of physical wireless channels. In realistic collaborative sensing scenarios, spectral resources are quantized into finite physical resource blocks or orthogonal subcarriers, rendering simultaneous transmission by all agents infeasible. To address this, we propose Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK), a control architecture designed to sustain robust, risk-aware coordination under strict instantaneous bandwidth caps. We introduce Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG), a lightweight coordination mechanism that enforces hard access constraints by scheduling only the top-K agents based on locally computed semantic importance scores. By aggregating these prioritized observations into a compact latent state, a self-supervised global encoder enables a distributional policy to mitigate tail risks despite data sparsity. We evaluate MASK across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that it matches the performance of communication-unconstrained baselines even when channel access is restricted to a small fraction of the swarm size. Furthermore, the framework exhibits inherent resilience to packet erasures, validating semantic scheduling as a critical enabler for resource-constrained 6G systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Soft Lamprey-Inspired Dual Environment Robot (SLIDER) and a proper modeling and optimization procedure employed to design the robot. We represent the primary fluid environment actions - inertial effects, vortex forces, and viscous dissipation - using Lighthill's theory for large-amplitude elongated bodies. For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [35] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Result: 在24个具身VLM基准中取得16项SOTA，超越Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5和GPT-5.4；微调为VLA后在4个操作基准套件中超越π₀.₅；零样本真实机器人实验在指令跟随、affordance grounding、铰接物体操作和长程复杂任务中表现优异。
